\begin{lexlean}{ReasoningFixtures}
\useglossary{lexlean.std.nat@1.2.0}
\importmodule{TargetSyntax}
\importmodule{TargetSemantics}
\importmodule{ReasoningOracle}
\importmodule{GradeOracle}
\importmodule{BudgetOracle}
\importmodule{PlannerOracle}
\importmodule{ScreeningOracle}
\title{Natural number addition}
\begin{semanticmodule}
definition{
  name: encodeFailure,
  parameters: [failure : ReasoningFailure],
  result: TargetSyntax::Value,
  body: (match failure
    | ReasoningFailure.exhausted => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool false) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool false))
    | ReasoningFailure.unsolved => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool false) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool true))
    | ReasoningFailure.rejected => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool true) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool false))
    | ReasoningFailure.invalid_step => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool true) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool true)))
}

definition{
  name: encodeTriageStep,
  parameters: [step : ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step],
  result: TargetSyntax::Value,
  body: (match step
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Fever => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 0 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Tachycardia => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 1 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Tachypnea => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 2 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Leukocytosis => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 3 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Sirs => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 4 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Sepsis => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 5 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Shock => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 6 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step.Escalate => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 7 (nil TargetSyntax::Value)))
}

definition{
  name: encodeTriageSteps,
  parameters: [steps : (List ReasoningOracle::Triage.Step)],
  result: (List TargetSyntax::Value),
  body: (match steps
    | List.nil => (nil TargetSyntax::Value)
    | List.cons step rest => (cons (encodeTriageStep step) (encodeTriageSteps rest))),
  recursive_argument: steps
}

definition{
  name: encodePlanStepsOne,
  parameters: [step : PlannerOracle::Plan.Step],
  result: TargetSyntax::Value,
  body: (match step
    | PlannerOracle::Plan.Step.FillA => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 0 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | PlannerOracle::Plan.Step.EmptyB => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 1 (nil TargetSyntax::Value))
    | PlannerOracle::Plan.Step.Pour carried => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 2 (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat carried) (nil TargetSyntax::Value))))
}

definition{
  name: encodePlanSteps,
  parameters: [steps : (List PlannerOracle::Plan.Step)],
  result: (List TargetSyntax::Value),
  body: (match steps
    | List.nil => (nil TargetSyntax::Value)
    | List.cons step rest => (cons (encodePlanStepsOne step) (encodePlanSteps rest))),
  recursive_argument: steps
}

definition{
  name: encodeScreenStepsOne,
  parameters: [step : ScreeningOracle::Screen.Step],
  result: TargetSyntax::Value,
  body: (match step
    | ScreeningOracle::Screen.Step.Propose carried => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 0 (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat carried) (nil TargetSyntax::Value))))
}

definition{
  name: encodeScreenSteps,
  parameters: [steps : (List ScreeningOracle::Screen.Step)],
  result: (List TargetSyntax::Value),
  body: (match steps
    | List.nil => (nil TargetSyntax::Value)
    | List.cons step rest => (cons (encodeScreenStepsOne step) (encodeScreenSteps rest))),
  recursive_argument: steps
}

definition{
  name: encodeDoseStepsOne,
  parameters: [step : ScreeningOracle::Dose.Step],
  result: TargetSyntax::Value,
  body: (match step
    | ScreeningOracle::Dose.Step.candidate carried => (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.adt 0 (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat carried) (nil TargetSyntax::Value))))
}

definition{
  name: encodeDoseSteps,
  parameters: [steps : (List ScreeningOracle::Dose.Step)],
  result: (List TargetSyntax::Value),
  body: (match steps
    | List.nil => (nil TargetSyntax::Value)
    | List.cons step rest => (cons (encodeDoseStepsOne step) (encodeDoseSteps rest))),
  recursive_argument: steps
}

  name: reasoningDose120Run,

  body: (TargetSemantics::run 4000 (reasoningDose120Program) 0 (cons (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat 120) (nil TargetSyntax::Value))),
  axioms: [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext]
}

  name: reasoningDose120Outcome,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((reasoningDose120Run) = (ctor TargetSemantics::Outcome.value (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.error (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool false) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.bool false))) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat 3) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat 3) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat 0) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat 0) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat 3) (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.nat 0))))))) 609)),

theorem{
  name: reasoningDose120Agrees,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((reasoningDose120Run) = (ctor TargetSemantics::Outcome.value (ctor TargetSyntax::Value.pair (match (ScreeningOracle::Dose 120)
